res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 28,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 36,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 44,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 52,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.PenSize(0.4)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 60,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 70,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)


res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+14,50,0)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+64.6,50,0)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+90,75,0)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+117,50,0)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+144,50,0)

res = pe1.PenSize(0.4)
res = pe1.Box(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin,75,SagMargin-SolMargin)

End sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
res = pe1.nopen() 
'*******************
FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.NoPen()
res = pe1.Write(UstMargin - 5,SolMargin + 2, 6,180,FirmaAdi)
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10) 
res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
MPSRefNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1) 
MamulKodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
MamulAdi = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",MamulKodu,-1)
UretimSipNo = MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
GrupNo = MMPS10E.GRUPEVRAKNO(1)
ToleransTipi = MMPS10E.TOLERANSTYPE(1)
Tolerans = MMPS10E.TOLERANS(1)

    Set tarih2=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
    tarih2.SetDate(MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(1))
    degisken2=tarih2.GetDowStr()
    if degisken2="Paz" then 
         TeslimTarihi = Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(1))-1)
    else 
         TeslimTarihi=Doc.StrtoNorDate(MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(1))
    end if

    Set TeslimTarihi2=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
    TeslimTarihi2.SetDate(TeslimTarihi)
    TeslimTarihiAdi=TeslimTarihi2.GetDowStr()
    select case TeslimTarihiAdi
      Case "Paz"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="PAZAR"
      Case "Pzt"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="PAZARTESÝ"
      Case "Sal"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="SALI"
      Case "Çar"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="ÇARÞAMBA"
      Case "Per"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="PERÞEMBE"
      Case "Cum"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="CUMA"
      Case Else
         TeslimTarihiAdi="CUMARTESÝ"
    End Select

MamulMiktari = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(MamulMiktari)
MamulMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(MamulMiktari,18,Ondalik) 
StokBirimi = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",MamulKodu,-1) 
MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
MusteriAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriKodu,-1)
MusteriAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",MusteriKodu,-1)
MusteriAdi = trim(MusteriAdi) & " " & trim(MusteriAdi2)
Not1 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1(1)
Not2 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2(1) 
MPSGKod= MMPS10E.GK_1(1)
MusTeslimTarihi = Doc.StrtoNorDate(MMPS10E.MUSTERIYETESTARIH(1)) 
bBasla = UstMargin+7
SatirAra = 6
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"MPS Ref No")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 44, 6,2,": ")
res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 46, 6,85,trim(MPSRefNo)) 
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"Mamul")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 44,6,2,": ")
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 46,6,SagMargin - SolMargin - 40,MamulKodu & "" & MamulAdi)
' res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
' res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 41, 6,85,MamulAdi)
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"Mamul Miktarý")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 44,6,2,": ")
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 46, 6,45,MamulMiktari & " " & trim(StokBirimi))


res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"Üretimden Teslim Tarihi")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 44, 6,2,": ")
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 46, 6,85,TeslimTarihi&" "&TeslimTarihiAdi) 

res = pe1.SetBold(1) 
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"Notlar")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 44, 6,2,": ")
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 46, 6,SagMargin - SolMargin - 50,Not1)
If trim(Not2) <> "" Then
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 46, 6,SagMargin - SolMargin - 50,Not2)
End If
Alt = pe1.GetBottom() 
res = pe1.Box(BBasla - 2, SolMargin, Alt - BBasla + 2,SagMargin - SolMargin)
res = pe1.pensize(0.3) 
res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT") 
res = pe1.pensize(0.4)

Call Normal(pe1)


End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
'sss
If trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
rSiraNo = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(SatirNo) 
Ekler = SatirSatir(SatirNo) + Muhendisliksatir(SatirNo) + MesajSatir(SatirNo) + HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo)
sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom() + Ekler 
If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
siraNo = 1
res = pe1.PageBreak()
' Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
satirpozr = UstMargin + 6
Else
If SiraNo = 0 Then
satirpozr = satirBaslangic
Else
satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
' satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
End If
siraNo = siraNo + 1
End If 
satirBas = satirPozr 
TezgahKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
TezgahAdi = Doc.GetKartValue("IMLT00","AD",TezgahKodu,-1)
OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetKartValue("IMLT01","AD",OperasyonKodu,-1)
MamulPaket = MMPS10T.R_YMK_MPAKET(SatirNo)
YMamulPaket = MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKET(SatirNo)
YMPaketIcerigi = MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(SatirNo)
YMMiktari = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(SatirNo) 
Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(YMMiktari)
YMMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(YMMiktari,18,Ondalik)
Ondalik1 = Doc.SigDig(YMPaketIcerigi)
YMPaketIcerigi = Doc.NumFormat(YMPaketIcerigi,18,Ondalik1) 
OperasyonSuresi =MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(SatirNo)
OperasyonSuresi =Doc.NumFormat(OperasyonSuresi,12,1) 
res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
res = pe1.PenSize(0.3) 
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
satirPozr = satirPozr + 2 
Blok2 = SolMargin + 95 
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, SolMargin + 2,5,28,"Ýþ Merkezi")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, SolMargin + 30,5,Blok2 - SolMargin - 32,": " & trim(TezgahKodu)& "   " &trim(TezgahAdi)) 
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, Blok2,5,25,"Üretim Miktarý")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
UretimMiktari = trim(MamulPaket) & " x " & trim(YMamulPaket) & " x " & trim(YMPaketIcerigi) & _
" = " & trim(YMMiktari)
res = pe1.WriteRTF(SatirPozr, Blok2 + 26,5,SagMargin - Blok2 - 28,"\b1 : \b0 " & UretimMiktari) 
If trim(OperasyonKodu) <> "" Then
satirPozr = pe1.GetTop() + SatirYuksekligi
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2,5,28,"Operasyon")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, SolMargin + 30,5,Blok2 - SolMargin - 32,": " & OperasyonAdi) 
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, Blok2,5,25,"Oper. Süresi")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, Blok2 + 26,5,3,":")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, Blok2 + 29,5,15,OperasyonSuresi)
res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, Blok2 + 46,5,12,"SAAT")
End If
rSiraNo = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(SatirNo)
SatirSon = pe1.GetBottom() 
SatirPozr = SatirPozr + 2 
Call MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, SatirNo)
Call MesajYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, SatirNo)
Call HammaddeYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, rSiraNo)
SatirSon = SatirSon + Muhendisliksatir(SatirNo) + MesajSatir(SatirNo) + 2 
HammaddeSon = SatirSon + HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo) + 2 
'xxx
res = pe1.Box(SatirBas ,SolMargin,SatirSon - SatirBas -1,SagMargin - SolMargin)
If HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo) > 0 Then
res = pe1.Box(SatirSon-1,SolMargin,HammaddeSon - SatirSon+1,SagMargin - SolMargin)
End If
' amman dikkat bu satýr önemli! ***********
res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom() - 2,2,3,5,"")
'****************************************** 
End Sub

Function SatirSatir(satirNo)
OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo) 
If trim(OperasyonKodu) <> "" Then
k = 4
Else 
k = 2
End If
satirSatir = k * SatirYuksekligi
End Function

Sub MesajYaz(pe1,SatirPozr, SatirNo)
'mmm
rows = Doc.SelectEQ("GNMESG","KRITER+KRITER_V+TRNUM","UP**"+MMPS10T.JOBNO(SatirNo)+Space(6))
Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_GNMESG_SELECT")
For i = 1 To rows
MesajKodu = Old.KOD(i)
If trim(MesajKodu) = "FILE" Then
Dosya = Old.Notes(i)
If i = 1 Then
satirPozr = SatirPozr() + Satiryuksekligi
Else
satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
End If
res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
res = pe1.NoPen()
res = pe1.Picture(SatirPozr, Solmargin + 30, 50,120,"..\wd\images\" & trim(Dosya),114)
res = pe1.UseSet(1)
Else
If i = 1 Then
satirPozr = SatirPozr + Satiryuksekligi
Else
satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
End If
Mesaj = Old.NOTES(i) 
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 30, 5,120,Mesaj)
End If
Next
End Sub

Function MesajSatir(SatirNo)
'mmm
rows = Doc.SelectEQ("GNMESG","KRITER+KRITER_V+TRNUM","UP**"+MMPS10T.JOBNO(SatirNo)+Space(6))
Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_GNMESG_SELECT")
For i = 1 To rows
MesajKodu = Old.KOD(i)
If trim(MesajKodu) = "FILE" Then
k = k + 55
Else
k = k + SatirYuksekligi
End If
Next
MesajSatir = k
End Function

Sub MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, SatirNo)
'ttt
' Doc.Tech10_select(mamulkodu,yarimamulkodu,kaynaktype,kaynakkodu,operasyon,musterikodu,mpsno)
MamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU(SatirNo)
YariMamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULKODU(SatirNo)
KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(SatirNo)
KaynakKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1) 
res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)
Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
SatirSayisi = Tech.GetRecCount()
res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
Tanim = Tech.TSTEXT(i) 
satirPozr = satirPozr + Satiryuksekligi
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 30, 5,SagMargin - SolMargin - 32,Tanim)
Next
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub

Function Muhendisliksatir(SatirNo)
'ttt
' Doc.Tech10_select(mamulkodu,yarimamulkodu,kaynaktype,kaynakkodu,operasyon,musterikodu,mpsno)
MamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU(SatirNo)
YariMamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULKODU(SatirNo)
KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(SatirNo)
KaynakKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1) 
res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo) 
Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT") 
MuhendislikSatir = Tech.GetRecCount() * SatirYuksekligi 
End Function

Sub HammaddeYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, rSiraNo)
'hhh
SatirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
rSiraNo1 = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i)
If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)) = "H" Then
SatirPozr = SatirPozr + Satiryuksekligi 
HammaddeKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
HammaddeAdi = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",HammaddeKodu,-1)
HammaddeMiktari = MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(i)
HammaddeMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(HammaddeMiktari,18,Doc.SigDig(HammaddeMiktari))
HammaddeBirim = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,-1) 
res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 2, 5,40,HammaddeKodu)
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 44, 5,87,HammaddeAdi)
res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 132, 5,15,HammaddeMiktari)
res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 149, 5,12,Hammaddebirim)
res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 161, 5,30,"..............") 
End If
Next
End Sub

Function HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo)
'hhh
SatirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
rSiraNo1 = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i)
If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)) = "H" Then
n = n + 1
End If
Next
HammaddeSatir = n * SatirYuksekligi
End Function 

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
res = pe1.Write(Alt+2, Solmargin+2,6,32,"FR-75-03 R(2)")
End Sub

Sub SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
sayfaSayisi = pe1.GetPageCount()
For i = 1 To SayfaSayisi
res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
res = pe1.WriteRTF(UstMargin , SagMargin - 30, 6,30, "\b Sayfa : \b0 " & i & " / " & SayfaSayisi)
Next
End Sub 

Sub Normal(pe1)
res = pe1.setbold(0)
res = pe1.setitalic(0)
res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
res = pe1.setrotation(0)
res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ARKO10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MMPS10-10
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS301
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Ýþ Emri Formu - 7 - Toplu Basým (Operatörler Ýçin Tablo Ýlaveli)
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, MMPS10E, MMPS10T
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()

	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 15
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 15
	satiryuksekligi = 5
'	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 64
	satirSonu = 285

	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Ýþ Emri", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	For j = 1 to MMPS30T.GetRecCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(MMPS30T.Selected(j)) then
			siraNo = 0        
			Rows = Doc.SelectEQ("MMPS10E","EVRAKNO",MMPS30T.EVRAKNO(j))
			Set MMPS10E = Doc.getTableObject("AU_MMPS10E_SELECT")

			Rows = Doc.SelectEQ("MMPS10T","EVRAKNO",MMPS30T.EVRAKNO(j))
			Set MMPS10T = Doc.getTableObject("AU_MMPS10T_SELECT")

			if j <> 1 then res = pe1.PageBreak

			BaslangicSayfa= pe1.GetPageCount
			satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			SatirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
			For i = 1 To satirSayisi
				KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)
				If KaynakTipi = "I" Then
					Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
				End If
          if i=satirSayisi then
             Call TablomCiz(pe1)
          end if
			Next
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			sonSayfa = pe1.GetPageCount
			Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1, BaslangicSayfa, sonSayfa)
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub TablomCiz(pe1)

satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom()

If (satirPozr + 91) > satirSonu Then
 res = pe1.PageBreak()
 satirPozr = UstMargin + 6
End If

res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 11,SolMargin,4,14, "SIRA")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 15,SolMargin,4,14, "NO")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 13,SolMargin+14,6,45.6, "OPERATÖR")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 13,SolMargin+64.6,6,25.4, "TARÝH")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 13,SolMargin+90,6,27, "VARDÝYA")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 11,SolMargin+117,4,27, "ÜRETÝLEN")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 15,SolMargin+117,4,27, "MÝKTAR")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 13,SolMargin+144,6,36, "ÝMZA")

res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 22,SolMargin,6,14, "1")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 30,SolMargin,6,14, "2")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 38,SolMargin,6,14, "3")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 46,SolMargin,6,14, "4")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 54,SolMargin,6,14, "5")

res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 62,SolMargin,6,(SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "HAZIRLAYAN")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 62,(SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2,6,(SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "ONAYLAYAN")
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)

res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 72,SolMargin+2,6,50, "ADI SOYADI:")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 78,SolMargin+2,6,50, "ÝMZA      :")

res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 72,SolMargin+92,6,50, "ADI SOYADI:")
res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 78,SolMargin+92,6,50, "ÝMZA      :")

res = pe1.PenSize(0.4)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 20,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 28,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 36,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 44,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 52,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.PenSize(0.4)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 60,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 70,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)


res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+14,50,0)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+64.6,50,0)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+90,75,0)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+117,50,0)
res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin+144,50,0)

res = pe1.PenSize(0.4)
res = pe1.Box(satirPozr + 10,SolMargin,75,SagMargin-SolMargin)

End sub


Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
'*******************
		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin - 5,SolMargin + 2, 6,180,FirmaAdi)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		MPSRefNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)

		MamulKodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
		MamulAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",MamulKodu,"")
		UretimSipNo = MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
		GrupNo = MMPS10E.GRUPEVRAKNO(1)
		ToleransTipi = MMPS10E.TOLERANSTYPE(1)
		Tolerans = MMPS10E.TOLERANS(1)
	

    Set tarih2=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
    tarih2.SetDate(MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(1))
    degisken2=tarih2.GetDowStr()
    if degisken2="Paz" then 
         TeslimTarihi = Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(1))-1)
    else 
         TeslimTarihi=Doc.StrtoNorDate(MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(1))
    end if

    Set TeslimTarihi2=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
    TeslimTarihi2.SetDate(TeslimTarihi)
    TeslimTarihiAdi=TeslimTarihi2.GetDowStr()
    select case TeslimTarihiAdi
      Case "Paz"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="PAZAR"
      Case "Pzt"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="PAZARTESÝ"
      Case "Sal"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="SALI"
      Case "Çar"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="ÇARÞAMBA"
      Case "Per"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="PERÞEMBE"
      Case "Cum"
         TeslimTarihiAdi="CUMA"
      Case Else
         TeslimTarihiAdi="CUMARTESÝ"
    End Select

     MusTeslimTarihi = Doc.StrtoNorDate(MMPS10E.MUSTERIYETESTARIH(1)) 
		MamulMiktari = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
		Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(MamulMiktari)
		MamulMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(MamulMiktari,18,Ondalik)
	
		StokBirimi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",MamulKodu,"")

		MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		MusteriAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriKodu,"")
		MusteriAdi2 = doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","NAME2",MusteriKodu,"")
		MusteriAdi = trim(MusteriAdi) & " " & trim(MusteriAdi2)
		Not1 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1(1)
		Not2 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2(1)
     
		bBasla = UstMargin+7
		SatirAra = 6
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"MPS Ref No")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 44, 6,2,": ")
res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 46, 6,85,trim(MPSRefNo)) 
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"Mamul")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 44,6,2,": ")
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 46,6,SagMargin - SolMargin - 40,MamulKodu & "" & MamulAdi)
' res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
' res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 41, 6,85,MamulAdi)
res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"Mamul Miktarý")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 44,6,2,": ")
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 46, 6,45,MamulMiktari & " " & trim(StokBirimi))

res = pe1.SetBold(1)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"Üretimden Teslim Tarihi")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 44, 6,2,": ")
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 46, 6,85,TeslimTarihi&" "&TeslimTarihiAdi) 

res = pe1.SetBold(1) 
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,41,"Notlar")
res = pe1.SetBold(0)
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 44, 6,2,": ")
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 46, 6,SagMargin - SolMargin - 50,Not1)
If trim(Not2) <> "" Then
res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 46, 6,SagMargin - SolMargin - 50,Not2)
End If

		Alt = pe1.GetBottom()

		res = pe1.Box(BBasla - 2, SolMargin, Alt - BBasla + 2,SagMargin - SolMargin)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")		

		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
'sss
	If trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	rSiraNo = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(SatirNo)

	Ekler = SatirSatir(SatirNo) + Muhendisliksatir(SatirNo) + MesajSatir(SatirNo) + HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo)
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom() + Ekler
	
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
'		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = UstMargin + 6
	Else
		If SiraNo = 0 Then
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
				satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
'				satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If		

	satirBas = satirPozr

	TezgahKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
	TezgahAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",TezgahKodu,"")
	OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
	OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","AD",OperasyonKodu,"")
	MamulPaket = MMPS10T.R_YMK_MPAKET(SatirNo)
	YMamulPaket = MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKET(SatirNo)
	YMPaketIcerigi = MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(SatirNo)
	YMMiktari = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(SatirNo)

	Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(YMMiktari)
	YMMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(YMMiktari,18,Ondalik)
	Ondalik1 = Doc.SigDig(YMPaketIcerigi)
	YMPaketIcerigi = Doc.NumFormat(YMPaketIcerigi,18,Ondalik1)

	OperasyonSuresi = MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(SatirNo)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	satirPozr = satirPozr + 2

	Blok2 = SolMargin  + 95

	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, SolMargin + 2,5,28,"Ýþ Merkezi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, SolMargin + 30,5,Blok2 - SolMargin - 32,": " & trim(TezgahKodu)& "   " &trim(TezgahAdi))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, Blok2,5,25,"Üretim Miktarý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	UretimMiktari = trim(MamulPaket) & " x " & trim(YMamulPaket) & " x " & trim(YMPaketIcerigi) & _
								" = " & trim(YMMiktari)
		res = pe1.WriteRTF(SatirPozr, Blok2 + 26,5,SagMargin - Blok2 - 28,"\b1 :  \b0 " & UretimMiktari)


	If trim(OperasyonKodu) <> "" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop() + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2,5,28,"Operasyon")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, SolMargin + 30,5,Blok2 - SolMargin - 32,": " & OperasyonAdi)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, Blok2,5,25,"Oper. Süresi")
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, Blok2 + 26,5,3,":")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, Blok2 + 29,5,15,OperasyonSuresi)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, Blok2 + 46,5,12,"SAAT")
	End If
	rSiraNo = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(SatirNo)
	SatirSon = pe1.GetBottom()

	SatirPozr = SatirPozr + 2

	Call MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, SatirNo)
	Call MesajYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, SatirNo)
	Call HammaddeYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, rSiraNo)
	SatirSon = SatirSon + Muhendisliksatir(SatirNo) + MesajSatir(SatirNo) + 2

	HammaddeSon = SatirSon + HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo) + 2
	
'xxx
	res = pe1.Box(SatirBas ,SolMargin,SatirSon - SatirBas -1,SagMargin - SolMargin)
	If HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo) > 0 Then
		res = pe1.Box(SatirSon-1,SolMargin,HammaddeSon - SatirSon+1,SagMargin - SolMargin)
	End If
' amman dikkat bu satýr önemli! ***********
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom() - 2,2,3,5,"")
'******************************************

End Sub
Function SatirSatir(satirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)

	If trim(OperasyonKodu) <> "" Then
		k = 4
	Else 
		k = 2
	End If
	satirSatir = k * SatirYuksekligi
End Function
Sub MesajYaz(pe1,SatirPozr, SatirNo)
'mmm
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("GNMESG","KRITER+KRITER_V+TRNUM","UP**"+MMPS10T.JOBNO(SatirNo)+Space(6))
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_GNMESG_SELECT")
	For i = 1 To rows
		MesajKodu = Old.KOD(i)
		If trim(MesajKodu) = "FILE" Then
			Dosya = Old.Notes(i)
			If i = 1 Then
				satirPozr = SatirPozr() + Satiryuksekligi
			Else
				satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
			End If
			res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
			res = pe1.NoPen()
			res = pe1.Picture(SatirPozr, Solmargin + 30, 50,120,"..\wd\images\" & trim(Dosya),114)
			res = pe1.UseSet(1)
		Else
			If i = 1 Then
				satirPozr = SatirPozr + Satiryuksekligi
			Else
				satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
			End If
			Mesaj = Old.NOTES(i) 
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 30, 5,120,Mesaj)
		End If
	Next
End Sub
Function MesajSatir(SatirNo)
'mmm
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("GNMESG","KRITER+KRITER_V+TRNUM","UP**"+MMPS10T.JOBNO(SatirNo)+Space(6))
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_GNMESG_SELECT")
	For i = 1 To rows
		MesajKodu = Old.KOD(i)
		If trim(MesajKodu) = "FILE" Then
			k = k + 55
		Else
			k = k + SatirYuksekligi
		End If
	Next
	MesajSatir = k
End Function
Sub MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, SatirNo)
'ttt
'	Doc.Tech10_select(mamulkodu,yarimamulkodu,kaynaktype,kaynakkodu,operasyon,musterikodu,mpsno)
	MamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	YariMamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(SatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)
	Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
	SatirSayisi = Tech.GetRecCount()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
		Tanim = Tech.TSTEXT(i)		
		satirPozr = satirPozr + Satiryuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 30, 5,SagMargin - SolMargin - 32,Tanim)
	Next
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub
Function Muhendisliksatir(SatirNo)
'ttt
'	Doc.Tech10_select(mamulkodu,yarimamulkodu,kaynaktype,kaynakkodu,operasyon,musterikodu,mpsno)
	MamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	YariMamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(SatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)

	Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")

	MuhendislikSatir = Tech.GetRecCount() * SatirYuksekligi

End Function
Sub HammaddeYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, rSiraNo)
'hhh
	SatirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
		rSiraNo1 = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i)
		If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And 	trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)) = "H"  Then
			SatirPozr = SatirPozr + Satiryuksekligi

			HammaddeKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
			HammaddeAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",HammaddeKodu,"")
			HammaddeMiktari = MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(i)
			HammaddeMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(HammaddeMiktari,18,Doc.SigDig(HammaddeMiktari))
			HammaddeBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,"")
		
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 2, 5,40,HammaddeKodu)
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 44, 5,87,HammaddeAdi)
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 132, 5,15,HammaddeMiktari)
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 149, 5,12,Hammaddebirim)
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 161, 5,30,"..............")

		End If
	Next
End Sub
Function HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo)
'hhh
	SatirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
		rSiraNo1 = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i)
		If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And 	trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)) = "H"  Then
			n = n + 1
		End If
	Next
	HammaddeSatir = n * SatirYuksekligi
End Function

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
'	res = pe1.Write(Alt, SagMargin - 30,6,32,"FR 343-01")
End Sub
Sub Hazirlayan(pe1,SatirPozr)
	If (satirPozr + 30) > satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		satirPozr = UstMargin + 6
	End If
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 5,SolMargin,6,(SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "HAZIRLAYAN")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 5,(SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2,6,(SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "ONAYLAYAN")
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.4)
	res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 3,(SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2,30,0)
	res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 12,SolMargin,0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Box(satirPozr + 3,SolMargin,30,SagMargin-SolMargin)
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1, BaslangicSayfa, sonSayfa)
	SayfaSayisi = sonSayfa - BaslangicSayfa + 1
	For i = BaslangicSayfa To sonSayfa
		sayfaNo = i - BaslangicSayfa + 1
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.WriteRTF(UstMargin , SagMargin - 30, 6,30, "\b Sayfa  :  \b0 " & sayfaNo & "  /  " & SayfaSayisi)
	Next
End Sub

Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ARKO10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MMPS60
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Net Cývata Tel Çekme Ýþ Emri
Kod : 
Public Toplam(), GToplam, Alt, SiraNo, HammaddeKodu, HammaddeAdi
Sub PrintForm()
Dim pe1
ReDim Toplam(1)
Gtoplam = 0
Alt = 0
SiraNo = 0
HammaddeKodu = ""
HammaddeAdi = ""
   Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
   res = pe1.Open(0,"Baþlýk",297,210,"")
   Set MMPS60E = doc.GetTableObject("MMPS60E")
   Set MMPS10T = doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
   Set MMPS60T = doc.GetTableObject("MMPS60T")
   Set MMPS10E = doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
   Set Notlar = doc.GetTableObject("NOTLAR")
   Call SayfaBasi(pe1,MMPS60T,MMPS60E)
   satirSayisi = MMPS60T.GetRecCount()
   For i=1 To satirSayisi
      Call SatirYazdir(pe1,MMPS60T,MMPS60E,i)
   Next	
   Call SayfaSonu(pe1,MMPS60T,MMPS60E)
	For k=0 To UBound(Toplam)-1
		GToplam = GToplam + Toplam(k)
   Next
	GToplam = FormatNumber(GToplam,1,-1)
	alt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",11)
	res = pe1.Box(alt+6,16,15,182)
	res = pe1.write(alt + 11,19,6,30,"TOPLAM : ")
	res = pe1.write(alt + 11,57,6,35,HammaddeKodu)
	res = pe1.write(alt + 11,92,6,50,HammaddeAdi)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",11)
	res = pe1.write(alt + 11 ,158,6,27,GToplam)
	HBirim = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,-1)
	res = pe1.write(alt + 11 ,186,6,10,HBirim)

	For ds=1 To Notlar.GetRecCount()
		Not1 = trim(Notlar.GetFieldValue("NOT1",ds))
		If Not1 <> "" Then
			DoluSatir = DoluSatir+1
		elseif Not1 = "" Then
			For ds1=ds To Notlar.GetRecCount()
				If trim(Notlar.GetFieldValue("NOT1",ds1)) <> "" Then
					kk=kk+1
				End If
			Next
			If kk>0 Then
				DoluSatir = DoluSatir+1
				kk=0
			End If
		End If		
	Next
	If pe1.getbottom() + DoluSatir*6 > 270 Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak()		   
		Call SB(pe1,MMPS60E,MMPS60T)
		alt = 40 
	End If
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	NSatir = alt + 24
	If DoluSatir<> 0 Then
		For n=1 To DoluSatir
			MPSNotu = Notlar.GetFieldValue("NOT1",n)
'			if MPSNotu <> "" then
				NSatir = Nsatir + 6 
				res = pe1.write(NSatir ,19,6,175,MPSNotu)		
'			end if
		Next
		EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()			 
		res=pe1.Box(alt+26,16,Enalt-Alt-25,182)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res=pe1.SetBkgMode(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.write(alt+24,19,6,20,"NOTLAR")		
	End If

	SayfaSayisi = pe1.GetPageCount()
	For i=1 To SayfaSayisi
		res=pe1.GoToPage(i)
		Baslik = Trim(CStr(i)) + " / " + Trim(CStr(pe1.GetPageCount()))	
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.WriteRTF(49,128,6,40,"\b SAYFA : \b0  " & Baslik) 	   
	Next   
   res = pe1.Show()
   res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub
Sub SatirYazdir(pe1,MMPS60T,MMPS60E,satirNo)
   satirYuksekligi = 15
   sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
   satirSonu = 230
	iSatir = 8
	KaynakTipi = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("R_KAYNAKTYPE",satirNo)

   If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
       siraNo = 1
		 Call SayfaSonu(pe1,MMPS60T,MMPS60E)       
       res = pe1.PageBreak()
'		 Alt = 0
       Call SayfaBasi(pe1,MMPS60T,MMPS60E)
       satirpozr = 79
   Else
		If KaynakTipi = "I" Then
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
			If siraNo = 1 Then
			    satirpozr = 79
			Else
			    satirpozr = pe1.Gettop() + satirYuksekligi - 10
			End If
		End If
   End If

	If KaynakTipi = "I" Then
		rSiraNo = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("R_SIRANO",satirNo)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",11)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,19,6,13,rSiraNo)

		MPSNo = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("EVRAKNO",satirNo)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",11)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,38,6,19,MPSNo)

		StokKodu = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("GetStrField('MMPS10E','MAMULSTOKKODU',EVRAKNO)",satirNo)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",10)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,64,6,35,StokKodu)

		StokAdi = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,-1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",10)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,100,6,55,StokAdi)

		Miktar = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("GetDblField('MMPS10E','SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR',EVRAKNO)",satirNo)
		Miktar = FormatNumber(Miktar,0,-1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",11)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("Right")
'		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,158,6,27,Miktar)

		Birim = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",StokKodu,-1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",11)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,186,6,10,Birim)

		OperasyonTarihi = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("GetStrField('MMPS10T','R_LATEST_B_TARIH',JOBNO,'JOBNO')",satirNo)
		OperasyonTarihi = cDate(OperasyonTarihi)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.WriteRTF(SatirPozr+iSatir,19,6,48,"\b OP. TARÝHÝ : \b0  " & OperasyonTarihi)

		OperasyonKodu=MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("GetStrField('MMPS10T','R_OPERASYON',JOBNO,'JOBNO')",satirNo)
		OperasyonAdi = doc.GetKartValue("IMLT01","AD",OperasyonKodu,-1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.WriteRTF(SatirPozr+iSatir,70,6,70,"\b OP: \b0  " & OperasyonAdi)

		For j=1 To MMPS60T.GetRecCount()
			rSiraNo1 = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("R_SIRANO",j)
			KaynakTipi1 = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("R_KAYNAKTYPE",j)
			MPSNo1 = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("EVRAKNO",j)
			If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And MPSNo1=MPSNo And KaynakTipi1 = "H" Then
				HammaddeKodu = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("R_KAYNAKKODU",j)
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",10)
				res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
				res = pe1.SetBold(0)
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr + 2*iSatir,19,6,35,HammaddeKodu)
				HammaddeAdi = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",HammaddeKodu,-1)
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",10)
				res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
				res = pe1.SetBold(0)
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr + 2*iSatir,57,6,35,HammaddeAdi)

				HammaddeMiktar = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("GetDblField('MMPS10T','R_MIKTAR0',JOBNO,'JOBNO')",j)
				HammaddeMiktar = FormatNumber(HammaddeMiktar,1,-1)
				ReDim preserve Toplam(satirNo)
				Toplam(SatirNo-1)=HammaddeMiktar
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
				res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
				res = pe1.SetBold(0)
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
'				res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr + 2*iSatir,158,6,27,HammaddeMiktar)
				res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,158,6,27,HammaddeMiktar)

				HammaddeBirim = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,-1)
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
				res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
				res = pe1.SetBold(0)
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'				res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr + 2*iSatir ,186,6,10,HammaddeBirim)
				res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,186,6,10,HammaddeBirim)
			End If
		Next
	   res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		UretimEmriNo = MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("GetStrField('MMPS10E','URETIMSIPINO',EVRAKNO)",1)
		SeriNo = doc.GetUDFValue("UP",MPSNo,"SERINO",0)
		res = pe1.WriteRTF(SatirPozr+iSatir,144,7,51,"\b SN : \b0  " & trim(UretimEmriNo) & " - " & trim(SeriNo))
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		LotNo = doc.GetUDFValue("UP",MPSNo,"LOTNO",0)
		res = pe1.WriteRTF(SatirPozr+ 2*iSatir ,96,6,70,"\b ÜRETÝCÝ ÞARJ NO : \b0  " & CSTR(LotNo))
		If satirNo <> MMPS60T.GetrecCount() And pe1.GetBottom() < SatirSonu Then
			If trim(MMPS60T.GetFieldValue("R_KAYNAKTYPE",satirNo+1)) <> "" Then
				res=pe1.line(pe1.GetBottom() ,16,0,182)
			End If
		End If
	End If
End Sub
Sub SayfaBasi(pe1,MMPS60T,MMPS60E)
   res = pe1.SelectFont("Times New Roman",14)
   res = pe1.SetBold(1)

Public TBorcTutar(), TAlacakTutar()
Sub PrintForm()
Dim pe1
	ReDim TBorcTutar(1)
	ReDim	TAlacakTutar(1)
   Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
   res = pe1.Open(0,"Mahsup Fiþi",279.4,210,"")
   Set MUHA20E = doc.GetTableObject("MUHA20E")
   Set MUHA20T = doc.GetTableObject("MUHA20T")
   Call SayfaBasi(pe1,MUHA20T,MUHA20E)
   satirSayisi = MUHA20T.GetRecCount()
   For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		If trim(MUHA20T.GetFieldValue("KOD",i))<>"" Then
			Call SatirYazdir(pe1,MUHA20T,MUHA20E,i)
		End If
	Next
   Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	For i = 0 To ubound(TAlacakTutar)-1
		TotalAlacak = TotalAlacak + TAlacakTutar(i)
	Next
	For i = 0 To ubound(TBorcTutar)-1
		TotalBorc = TotalBorc + TBorcTutar(i)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",11)
	res = pe1.Write(259,13,6,21,"TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",8)
	res = pe1.line(257,10,0,190)
	res = pe1.Write(259,131,6,33,FormatNumber(TotalAlacak,0,,,-1))		
	res = pe1.Write(259,166,6,33,FormatNumber(TotalBorc,0,,,-1))
	res = pe1.Show()
   res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub
Sub SatirYazdir(pe1,MUHA20T,MUHA20E,satirNo)
   satirYuksekligi = 6
   sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
   satirSonu = 255
   If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
       siraNo = 1
       res = pe1.PageBreak()
       Call SayfaBasi(pe1,MUHA20T,MUHA20E)
       satirpozr = 70
   Else
       If satirNo = 1 Then
           satirpozr = 70
       Else
           satirpozr = pe1.Gettop() + satiryuksekligi
       End If
       siraNo = siraNo + 1
   End If
   HesapKodu = MUHA20T.GetFieldValue("KOD",satirNo)
   res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",9)
   res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
   res = pe1.SetBold(0)
   res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
   res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,12,6,21,HesapKodu)
   Aciklama = MUHA20T.GetFieldValue("ACIKLAMA",satirNo)
   res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
   res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
   res = pe1.SetBold(0)
   res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
   res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,82,6,46,Aciklama)
   HesapAdi = MUHA20T.GetFieldValue("GetStrField('MUHA00','AD',KOD)",satirNo)
   res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
   res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
   res = pe1.SetBold(0)
   res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
   res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,37,6,41,HesapAdi)
   res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
   BorcTutar = MUHA20T.GetFieldValue("BORCFIYAT",satirNo)
	ReDim preserve TBorcTutar(SatirNo)
	TBorcTutar(SatirNo-1)=BorcTutar
	BorcTutar1 = FormatNumber(BorcTutar,0,,,-1)
   res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",8)
   res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
   res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	If BorcTutar<> 0 Then
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,131,6,33,BorcTutar1)
   End If
   AlacakTutar = MUHA20T.GetFieldValue("ALACAKFIYAT",satirNo)
	ReDim preserve TAlacakTutar(SatirNo)
	TAlacakTutar(SatirNo-1)=AlacakTutar
	AlacakTutar1 = FormatNumber(AlacakTutar,0,,,-1)
   res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",8)
   res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
   res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	If AlacakTutar<> 0 Then
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,166,6,33,AlacakTutar1)
	End If
   Tutar = BorcTutar + AlacakTutar
   res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
   res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
   res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'   res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,181,6,32,Tutar)
End Sub

Sub SayfaSonu(pe1,MUHA20T,MUHA20E)
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	SayfaS = pe1.GetPageCount()
	For i=1 To SayfaS
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.Write(32,173,6,25, ": " & i & "/" & SayfaS)
	Next
End Sub

Sub SayfaSonu(pe1,MUHA20T,MUHA20E)
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	SayfaS = pe1.GetPageCount()
	For i=1 To SayfaS
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.Write(32,250,6,25, ": " & i & "/" & SayfaS)
	Next
End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
Set tableT = doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31T")
Set tableE = doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
prev = tableE.GetFieldValue("ISPREV",0)

Set pe1=doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
If prev = 0 Then
res=pe1.Open(1,"Atölye Takip",150,100,"")
Else
res=pe1.Open(0,"Atölye Takip",150,100,"") 
End If
res=pe1.pensize(0.6)

FisNo = tableE.GetFieldValue("EVRAKNO",0)
StokKodu = tableE.GetFieldValue("STOK_CODE",0) 
Adi = tableE.GetFieldValue("GETSTRFIELD('STOK00','AD',STOK_CODE)",0)
FisNo = tableE.GetFieldValue("EVRAKNO",0)
StokKodu = tableE.GetFieldValue("STOK_CODE",0) 
Adi = tableE.GetFieldValue("GETSTRFIELD('STOK00','AD',STOK_CODE)",0)
Miktar = tableE.GetFieldValue("SF_MIKTAR",0)
Miktar1 = formatnumber(miktar,-1,,-2) 
Birim = tableE.GetFieldValue("SF_SF_UNIT",0)
Operasyon = tableE.GetFieldValue("OPERASYON",0)
OperasyonAdi = tableE.GetFieldValue("GETSTRFIELD('IMLT01','AD',OPERASYON)",0)
SeriNo = tableE.GetFieldValue("LOTNUMBER",0) 
MpsNo = tableE.GetFieldValue("MPSNO",0) 
Notlar = tableE.GetFieldValue("NOTES",0) 
KaynakSayisi = tableT.GetRecCount()
res = pe1.nopen()
res = pe1.picture(5,64,12,30,"\d7k\wd\images\nexans.bmp",16)
res = pe1.selectFont("Arial",12)
res = pe1.setbold(1)
res = pe1.setbarcodeparms(2,2)
res = pe1.Barcode(5,5,10,40,"EAN128",trim(MpsNo),"")
res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
res = pe1.write(15,5,6,40,trim(MpsNo))
res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
res = pe1.selectFont("Arial",12)
res = pe1.write(24,5,7,90,Trim(Adi))
res = pe1.selectFont("Arial",12)
res = pe1.write(33,5,6,67,"Miktar : " & Miktar1 & " " & Trim(Birim))
res = pe1.nopen()
res = pe1.BarCode(39,15,10,50,"EAN128",Trim(Miktar),"")
res = pe1.selectFont("Arial",10)
res = pe1.write(53,5,12,70,OperasyonAdi)
res = pe1.setbarcodeparms(0,0)
res = pe1.BarCode(58,15,14,50,"EAN128",Trim(Operasyon),"")
res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
res = pe1.line(32,3,0,94) 
res = pe1.line(73,3,0,74) 
res = pe1.nopen()
res = pe1.write(74,5,4,49,"Notlar :") 
res = pe1.write(79,15,13,50, Notlar) 
res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
res = pe1.line(32,77,61.5,0) 
res = pe1.nopen()
res = pe1.BarCode(97,5,15,90,"EAN128",Trim(StokKodu),"") 
res = pe1.setrotation(270)
res = pe1.write(37,78,54,4,"Makara No")
res = pe1.BarCode(37,82,14,50,"EAN128",Trim(SeriNo),"")
res = pe1.setrotation(0) 
res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
res = pe1.box(117,3,29,94) 
res = pe1.write(119,5,5,50,"Giriþ Makaralarý :") 
res = pe1.box(22,3,71.5,94) 

k=1
For i=1 To 5
For j=1 To 3 
If k<=kaynaksayisi Then 
KSeriNo = tableT.getfieldvalue("LOTNUMBER",k)
res = pe1.write(124 + (i-1)*4.3,5+(j-1)*32,4,25,trim(KSeriNo)) 
k = k+1
End If
Next
Next 
If prev <> 0 Then
     res = pe1.Show()
End If
res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
If prev = 0 Then
res = pe1.printdoc(0)
res = pe1.close()
End If
End Sub

SUB PrintForm()

	Dim pe1
	SAYFABOYU 			=	297
	SAYFAENI 				= 	209
	HSATIRSONU			=	18
	KUTUSOLU				=	0
	KUTUSAGI				=	0
	KUTUALTI				= 	0
	KUTUUSTU				=	0
	IKIURUNISE			=	0
	ENALTTUMU				=	0
	ARTANSAYI				=	0
	SAYHAMSIRANO		=	0
	SATIRSONU		 		= 	28
	SATIRYUKSEKLIGI 	= 	4
	SAYI						=	0
	SIRANO 					= 	0
	USTMARGIN				= 	5
	ALTMARGIN 			= 	SAYFABOYU - 2
	SOLMARGIN 			= 	5
	SAGMARGIN				= 	SAYFAENI - 5
	SAGSINIR				=	203
		'ISEMRIENALTKONUM=21 YAPTIÐIMDA ÇÝZÝLEN ÝLK BOX ÜSTTEKÝ 16 cm LÝK ÝLK KUTU ÇERÇEVESÝNE DENK GELÝYOR.BÖYLECE ÇÝZGÝLER GÖRÜLMÜYOR.
	ISEMRIENALTKONUM	= 21
	m=0
	y=0
	my=0
	tk=0
	kk=0
	k=0
	yz=0
  	gj=0
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	ISEMRINO = Trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1))
	res = pe1.Open(0, "ÝÞEMRÝ", SAYFABOYU, SAYFAENI, "")
	If Doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & Doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	SIRANO = 0 
	SATIRSAYISI = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	Call PRINT_FORM_SAYFABASI(pe1,i)
	SATIRBASLANGIC = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
	MPSSAYFASEC=InputBox ("1- Tek Sayfa, 2- Çoklu Sayfa", "Ýþ Emri Tekli/Çoklu Sayfa Seçimi","1")
	If MPSSAYFASEC="" Then Exit Sub
	If Trim(MPSSAYFASEC) <> "1" Then
		Call PRINT_FORM_SATIRT(pe1, i)
		SATIRPOZR = pe1.GetBottom()
		Call PRINT_FORM_SAYFASONU(pe1)
	End If	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1,SATIRNO)

	rrt=39
	MPSREFNO			= MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1) 
	MAMULKODU 		= MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	MAMULADI 			= Trim(Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",MAMULKODU ,-1))
	MAMULADI2 		= Trim(Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","NAME2",MAMULKODU ,-1))
	NOT1 				= Trim(MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1(1))
	STOKBIRIMI	 	= Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",MAMULKODU ,-1) 
	LOGO=Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "IMAGES\opsLOGO.jpg"
 	FIRMAADI = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	RESIM_Y_MAMUL 	= Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\" & Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0P", "FULLFILENAME1", MAMULKODU +"000001", "EVRAKNO+SUBSRNUM"))
	RESIM_MAMUL 	= Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\" & Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0P", "FULLFILENAME1", MAMULKODU +"000002", "EVRAKNO+SUBSRNUM"))
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'EÐER ÝÞ EMRÝ PARÇASI MAMÜL ÝSE YARIMAMÜL ÝLE MAMÜL RESMÝNÝ EÞÝTLE
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select2("TTTX", "MMPS10TM", "", "", -1)
	ELDEEDILENURUN=Trim(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(1))
	If Left(ELDEEDILENURUN,1)="M" Then RESIM_MAMUL=RESIM_Y_MAMUL
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'**************************************************************
	Call KUTUCIZ(pe1)
	'***************************************************************
	KUTUSOLU=pe1.GetLeft+0.25
	KUTUSAGI=pe1.GetRight-0.25
	KUTUUSTU=pe1.GetTop+0.25
	KUTUALTI=pe1.GetBottom-0.25
	KUTUGENISLIGI=KUTUSAGI-KUTUSOLU
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(220,220,220))
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'LOGO
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(KUTUUSTU+1.5, KUTUSOLU, 12, 39, LOGO, 16)
		'LOGO SAÐI DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝ
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(KUTUUSTU, pe1.GetRight+0.5, 15, 0)
		'ÝÞ EMRÝ
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 27)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("Center")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+1.5, pe1.GetRight+0.5, 12, 67.2, "ÝÞ EMRÝ")
		'ÝÞEMRÝ SAÐI DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝ
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(KUTUUSTU, pe1.GetRight, 15, 0)
		'PARTÝ NO
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("Center")
	res = pe1.Write(KUTUUSTU, pe1.GetRight+0.05, 3.5, 30-0.05, "Parti No")
		'PARTÝ NO ALTI YATAY ÇÝZGÝ
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 0, 30)
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
		'PARTÝ NUMARASI
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("Center")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	If NOT1 = "" Then NOT1=Right(Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", ""),6)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom+1.5, pe1.GetLeft, 8, 30, Trim(NOT1))
		'PARTÝ NO SAÐI DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝ
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(KUTUUSTU, pe1.GetRight, 15, 0)
		'ÝÞEMRÝ NO BAÞLIK
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("Center")
	res = pe1.Write(KUTUUSTU, pe1.GetRight+0.05, 3.5, 30-0.05, "Ýþ Emri No")
		'ÝÞ EMRÝ NO ALTI YATAY ÇÝZGÝ
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 0, 30)
		'ÝÞEMRÝ NO
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("Center")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom+1.5, pe1.GetLeft, 8.5, 30, Trim(MPSREFNO))
		'ÝÞ EMRÝ SAÐI DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝ
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(KUTUUSTU, pe1.GetRight, 15, 0)
		'RESÝM 1
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)

		res = pe1.SetAlign("Left")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, STOKKODUSAGDIKCIZGISAGKONUM+1, SATIRYUKSEKLIGI-0.5, 105, Trim(MAMULADI) & " - [" & Trim(MAMULADI2) & "]" ) 
		res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)

			'HATCH ÇÝZGÝLERÝ
		res = pe1.SetHatchStyle("BDiagonal")

		res = pe1.SetHatchStyle("")
			'ELDE EDÝLEN (ÇOKLU) ÜRÜN ALT YATAY ÇÝZGÝ
		res = pe1.PenSize(0.1)

'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub PrintForm()

	CALL PrintLabel()

End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub PrintLabel()

	ETIKETSEC=""
	KULLANICI=Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	If KULLANICI = "TRM" Then 
		ETIKETSEC=3
	ElseIf KULLANICI	=	"TRM1" Then 
		ETIKETSEC=1
	Else
		ETIKETSEC	=	InputBox ("1- A5 SAYFADA 2 ETÝKET,                                     2- TABLOLU,                                                           3- BARKOD YAZICIYA", "BARKOD ETÝKET SEÇÝMÝ-SAYI GÝRÝN!","2")
	End If	
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------	
	If ETIKETSEC = "" Then Exit Sub
	If Left(Trim(ETIKETSEC),1)="2" Then
		SATIRYAZ=2
	ElseIf Left(Trim(ETIKETSEC),1)="1" Then
		SATIRYAZ=1
	ElseIf Left(Trim(ETIKETSEC),1)="3" Then
		SATIRYAZ=3
	Else
		SATIRYAZ=1
	End If
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	ETIKET="ETK-OPS2"
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	Set DOC1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set D7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	If Doc.GetDocType = "STOK20" Then
		Call STOK20_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK68" Then
		Call STOK68_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Call STOK60_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK25" Or Doc.GetDocType = "STOKFR2" Or Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		Call STOK25_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
		Call STOK29_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK36" Then
		Call STOK36_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK19" Then
		Call STOK19_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SAYM11" Then
		Call SAYM11_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK26" Then 
		Call Transfer_Print(STOK26T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK26" Then 
		'Call STOK25_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK63" Then
		Call Transfer_Print(STOK63T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK61" Then
		Call Transfer_Print(STOK61T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK24" Then
		Call STOK24_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MMPS10" Then
		Call MMPS10_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType= "TRACK01" Then
		Call TRACK01_Print()
	End if
	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "D7KIDSLB", ""

End Sub

			'----------------------------------------------------------------------------
			QQQ = "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'"
			Set M10TX = Doc.Select2("ddrr

Sub PrintForm()
Dim pe1,stok00T

	Set pe1=doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res=pe1.Open(0,"Stok Kartlarý",-1,-1,"LANDSCAPE")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.6)

   Set stok00T = doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	Call xxx(pe1,stok00t)
	
	res=pe1.Show()
	res=doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub

Sub xxx(pe1,stok00t)
	donemSayisi= doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","GERIVEILERIKACDONEME",-1)
	donemAraligi= doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","BIRDONEMKACGUN",-1)	
	baslangicTarihi = doc.strtonordate(doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","HESAPLAMATARIHI",-1))
	chartBaslik = trim(stok00T.getfieldValue("AD",0))
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res=pe1.Write(26,135,10,50,ChartBaslik)	

	bDonemlikHmdTalebi= doc.getfieldvalue("DT06","A000",1)
	bAcikSatinalmaSip= doc.getfieldvalue("DT06","A000",2)	
	bKalanHmmdSt= doc.getfieldvalue("DT06","A000",5)		


	veri=pe1.ChartDataCreate(3,donemSayisi)
	res=pe1.SetChartTitle("MRP")
	res=pe1.SetChartSubTitle(chartBaslik)
	res=pe1.SetChartFootNoteFontSizeFactor(0.8)
	res=pe1.SetChartFootnote("(Dönem Aralýðý : " & donemAraligi & " gün, Baþlangýç Tarihi : " & baslangicTarihi & ")")	
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri,0,rgb(0,0,255))
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri,1,rgb(32,208,40))
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri,2,rgb(255,0,0))
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetLineStyle(veri,2,1)
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetXAxisTitle(Veri,"Dönemler")
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetYAxisTitle(Veri,"(kg)")
	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri,bDonemlikHmdTalebi)	
	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri,bAcikSatinalmaSip)	
	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri,bKalanHmmdSt)	
	res=pe1.SetChartLegendPosition(0)
	res=pe1.SetChartXLabelStartValue(1)			

	For i= 1 To donemSayisi
		varDonem= "A" & right("00" & cstr(i) ,3)
		DonemlikHmdTalebi = doc.getfieldvalue("DT06",varDonem,1)
		res=pe1.ChartDataAddValue(veri,0,DonemlikHmdTalebi)			
		x = x + DonemlikHmdTalebi
	Next
	For i= 1 To donemSayisi
		varDonem= "A" & right("00" & cstr(i) ,3)
		AcikSatinalmaSip = doc.getfieldvalue("DT06",varDonem,2)
		res=pe1.ChartDataAddValue(veri,1,AcikSatinalmaSip)			
		y=y+AcikSatinalmaSip
	Next
	For i= 1 To donemSayisi
		varDonem= "A" & right("00" & cstr(i) ,3)
		KalanHmmdSt = doc.getfieldvalue("DT06",varDonem,5)
		res=pe1.ChartDataAddValue(veri,2,KalanHmmdSt)			
		z=KalanHmmdSt
	Next
	
'	res=pe1.chartDataSetHatchStyle(veri,0,2)
'	res=pe1.chartDataSetHatchStyle(veri,1,3)
'	res=pe1.chartDataSetHatchStyle(veri,2,4)


'	res=pe1.Chart(10,10, 190,280,veri,1)
'	res=pe1.SetChartGridRotation(900)
	res=pe1.SetChartGridBkgColor(rgb(255,255,220))
	res=pe1.SetChartGridBkgMode(0)
'	res=pe1.Chart(10,10, 190,280,veri,1)	
	res=pe1.Chart(10,10, 190,280,veri,2)	

	res=pe1.selectfont("Arial",9)
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(15,15,10,100,"Dönem Baþý Stok")	
	res=pe1.write(20,15,10,100,"Dönemlik Hammadde Talebi (Toplam) ")
	res=pe1.write(25,15,10,100,"Açýk Satýnalma Sipariþleri (Toplam) ")	
	res=pe1.write(30,15,10,100,"Dönem Sonu Hammadde Stoðu ")		
	res=pe1.write(15,75,10,5,": ")
	res=pe1.write(20,75,10,5,": ")	
	res=pe1.write(25,75,10,5,": ")		
	res=pe1.write(30,75,10,5,": ")
	
	k=doc.getfieldvalue("DT06","A001",5)
	
	res=pe1.setalign("right")
	
	k=formatnumber(k,2,,-2)
	x=formatnumber(x,2,,-2)
	y=formatnumber(y,2,,-2)
	z=formatnumber(z,2,,-2)

	res=pe1.write(15,78,10,20,k)
	res=pe1.write(20,78,10,20,x)	
	res=pe1.write(25,78,10,20,y)		
	res=pe1.write(30,78,10,20,z)

	res=pe1.setalign("left")
	res=pe1.write(15,99,10,10," kg")
	res=pe1.write(20,99,10,10," kg")	
	res=pe1.write(25,99,10,10," kg")		
	res=pe1.write(30,99,10,10," kg")		

End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ARKO10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK20-3
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üretim Fiþi - Esnek Form
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public xSutun1, xSutun2, xSutun3, xSutun4, xSutun5, xSutun6, xSutun7, xSutun8, xSutun9, xSutun10
Public wSutun1, wSutun2, wSutun3, wSutun4, wSutun5, wSutun6, wSutun7, wSutun8, wSutun9, wSutun10
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip
Public AlanSayisi()		 
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()

Sub PrintForm()
     Dim tableE, tableT
	ReDim AlanSayisi(100)		 
	ReDim xCizgi(100)
	ReDim Secilenler(100,3)
'	Set xCizgi = Nothing
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0

      sayfaTuru = InputBox("Sayfa Türünü Giriniz" & Chr(13) & "A - A4, L - Letter (Sürekli Form)", "Dinamo", "A")

      Select Case UCase(sayfaTuru)
              Case "L"
                      sayfaBoyu = 279.4
                      sayfaEni = 215.9
              Case "A"
                      sayfaBoyu = 297
                      sayfaEni = 210
              Case Else
                      K = MsgBox("A veya L giriniz", 48, "Uyarý")
      End Select					
        Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

        	Set tableE = doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
        	Set tableT = doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Call Alanlar(TableE,TableT)
		If ToplamEn > 1.1 * SayfaEni Then
			k = SayfaBoyu
			l = SayfaEni
			SayfaEni = k
			SayfaBoyu = l
			Tip = "LANDSCAPE"
			res = pe1.Open(0, "Üretim Fiþi", SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu, Tip)
		Else
			Tip = ""
			res = pe1.Open(0, "Üretim Fiþi", SayfaBoyu,SayfaEni , Tip)
		End If		

		BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
		satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 20

 		satirBaslangic = 70

		If doc.ErrorNumber Then
          	MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
			Exit Sub
        	End If
	Dim pe1
        siraNo = 0        
        Dim satirSayisi_tableT
        satirSayisi_tableT = tableT.GetRecCount()
        Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, tableE)
        For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
                Call Print_form_satir(pe1, tableT, I, tableE)
        Next
        
        Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1, tableE)

        res = pe1.Show()
        res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, evrak)
        res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
        res = pe1.nopen()
        res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
        res = pe1.box(8, 8, 30, SayfaEni-110)
        res = pe1.UseSet(1001)
        res = pe1.RemoveSet(1001)
        
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		kBasla = 10
		kAra = 9
        res = pe1.write(kBasla, SayfaEni - 62, 6, 50, "Form No         ")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 2* kAra , SayfaEni - 62, 6, 50, "Revizyon No     ")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 3 * kAra, SayfaEni - 62, 6, 50, "Revizyon Tarihi ")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 4 * kAra, SayfaEni - 62, 6, 50, "Fiþ No")
	   res = pe1.write(kBasla, SayfaEni - 32, 6, 50, ": KF 10-02")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 2 * kAra , SayfaEni - 32, 6, 50, ": 00")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 3 * kAra, SayfaEni - 32, 6, 50, ": 21.02.1997")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 4 * kAra, SayfaEni - 32, 6, 50, ": " & trim(evrak.getfieldValue("EVRAKNO",1)))
        res = pe1.nopen()
        res = pe1.setbarcodeparms(2, 2)
        res = pe1.BarCode(kBasla + 4, SayfaEni - 62, kAra, 50, "CODE39", "KF 10-02", "")

        res = pe1.nopen()
		If Tip = "LANDSCAPE" Then
	    	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
     		res = pe1.setbold(1)
	       	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
     	   	res = pe1.write(15, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 75, "Üretim Fiþi")
       	   	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	       	res = pe1.Picture(12, 10, 15, 55, "\d24\images\muhtese.jpg", 18)
		Else
	       	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
     	   	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	       	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
     	   	res = pe1.write(18, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 75, "Üretim Fiþi")
       	   	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	       	res = pe1.Picture(11, 10, 10, 35, "\d24\images\muhtese.jpg", 18)
		End If
        res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "12")
        res = pe1.setbold(1)
        res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
'        res = pe1.write(15.8, 18, 10, 45, firma)
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

        res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		BBasla = 30
		SatirAra = 5
'*******************
		
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(evrak.GetFieldValue("TARIH",1))
		Onay = evrak.GetFieldValue("ONAYEH1",1)
		FasonMu = evrak.GetFieldValue("FASONNORMAL",1)
		OtomatikRez = evrak.GetFieldValue("MPSSREZ1",1)
		MamulAmbarKodu = evrak.GetFieldValue("AMBCODE",1)
		MamulAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",MamulAmbarKodu,"")  
		ImAmbarKodu = evrak.GetFieldValue("IMALATAMBCODE",1)
		ImAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",ImAmbarKodu,"")  		
		YUAmbarKodu = evrak.GetFieldValue("YANMAMULAMBCODE",1)
		YUAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",YUAmbarKodu,"")  				
		NitelikKodu = Evrak.GetFieldValue("NITELIK",1)  
		Nitelik = doc.GetGecoValue("STKNIT","AD",NitelikKodu ,-1)

		res = pe1.write(BBasla , 12, 6, 33, "Tarih")
        res = pe1.write(BBasla , 45, 6, 20, ": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.write(BBasla, 100, 6, 25, "Fason/Normal")
        res = pe1.write(BBasla , 125, 6, 10, ": " & FasonMu)
		res = pe1.write(BBasla, 70, 6, 15, "Onay")
        res = pe1.write(BBasla , 85, 6, 10, ": " & Onay)
		res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, 12, 6, 33, "Mamul Ambar")
        res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, 45, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & trim(MamulAmbarKodu ) & "  " & Trim(MamulAmbarAdi))
		res = pe1.write(BBasla +2 *  SatirAra, 12, 6, 33, "Ýmalat Ambarý")
        res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, 45, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & trim(ImAmbarKodu) & "  " & Trim(ImAmbarAdi))
		res = pe1.write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, 12, 6, 33, "Yan Ürün Ambarý")
        res = pe1.write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, 45, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & trim(YUAmbarKodu) & "  " & Trim(YUAmbarAdi))
		res = pe1.write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, 12, 6, 33, "Nitelik")		
		res = pe1.write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, 45, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & Trim(NitelikKodu) & "  " & trim(Nitelik))
		
'***********************		
		Oran = BasilabilirAlan / ToplamEn

        res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		FontBoyu = FontAyarla(BasilabilirAlan,ToplamEn,10)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", FontBoyu)
			Sol = 12
			For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) / 3.22 * Oran
				Baslik = Grid(i,2)
				If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
					res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
					Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				End If
			Next

        res = pe1.pensize(0.6)

'köþedeki kutu
        res = pe1.Line(8, sayfaEni - 65, satirBaslangic - 22, 0)
        res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'        res = pe1.Line(46, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)

'yatay çizgiler
        res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
        res = pe1.Line(28, 10, 0, SayfaEni-75)
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
        res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 2, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
        res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
        res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
        res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
        Dim satiryuksekligi, x
        satiryuksekligi = 6

        Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(ByVal pe1, ByVal tableT, ByVal satirNo, ByVal tableE)
Dim satirpozr, satiryuksekligi
        satiryuksekligi = 6
        sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
        If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
                Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1, tableE)
                siraNo = 1
                res = pe1.PageBreak()
                Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, tableE)
                satirpozr = satirBaslangic
        Else
                If satirNo = 1 Then
                        satirpozr = satirBaslangic
                Else
                        satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
                End If
                siraNo = siraNo + 1
        End If
        
        res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
		FontBoyu = FontAyarla(BasilabilirAlan,ToplamEn,10)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", FontBoyu)
        res = pe1.setbold(0)
        Sol = 12
        res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)  / 3.22 * BasilabilirAlan / ToplamEn
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
'				If DegiskenAdi = "" Then Exit For
				Degisken = trim(cstr(TableT.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If IsNumeric(Degisken) And instr(1,DegiskenAdi,"KOD")=0 Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = DecimalHesapla(TableT,DegiskenAdi)
					Degisken = SayiBicimle(Degisken, Ondalik, 1, 1)
				Elseif IsDate(Degisken) Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
					res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
					Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
					xCizgi(j) =	Sol - 2
					j = j + 1
				End If	
			Next					 
        sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(ByVal pe1, ByVal evrak)

        res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'dýþ çerçeve
        res = pe1.box(8, 10, satirSonu - 3, SayfaEni - 20)

'dikey çizgiler
        res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) -1
		  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		  res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 19, 0)
		Next
'        Call imza(pe1)

'        Call Logo(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar(TableE,TableT)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","STOK20T")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		k = Grid(i,2)
		m =instr(1,k,"\n") 
		If m > 0 Then
			Gird(i,2)= Replace(1,Grid(i,2)," ")
	 	End If
	Next
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
	ToplamEn = ToplamEn / 3.22
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
        res = pe1.setbold(0)
        res = pe1.setitalic(0)
        res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
        res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
        res = pe1.setrotation(0)
        res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
        res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
        res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub
Function BosMu(Table,Field)
	k=0
  	satir = Table.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To Satir
		AlanDegeri = trim(Table.GetFieldValue(Field,i))
		If Isnumeric(AlanDegeri) Then
			If  Alandegeri <> 0  Then
				k = k + 1
			End If
		Else
			If  AlanDegeri <> "" Then
				k = k + 1
			End If
		End If
	Next
	BosMu = k
End Function
Function FontAyarla(BasilacakAlan,ToplamGen,Font)
	Oran = BasilacakAlan / ToplamGen
	If Oran >= 1 Then
		FontAyarla = Font
	Else
		If oran <=0.8 Then
			FontAyarla = Round(0.8 * Font,0)
		Else
			FontAyarla = Round(Oran * Font,0)
		End If
	End If
End Function
Function SayiBicimle(Sayi, OndalikSayisi, SondaSifir, Ayirac)
    If OndalikSayisi >= 0 Then
        Sayi = Round(Sayi, OndalikSayisi)
    End If
    Nokta = InStr(1, Sayi, ".")
    If Nokta = 0 Then
        ondalik = ""
    Else
        ondalik = Right(CStr(Sayi), Len(CStr(Sayi)) - Nokta)
    End If
    TamSayi = CStr(Fix(Sayi))
    If SondaSifir > 0 Then
        ondalik = SifirEkle(ondalik, OndalikSayisi)
    ElseIf SondaSifir = -1 Then
        ondalik = ondalik
    Else
        ondalik = SifirSil(ondalik)
    End If
    If Ayirac = 1 Then
        TamSayi = VirgulEkle(TamSayi)
    End If
    If ondalik = "" Then
        SayiBicimle = TamSayi
    Else
        SayiBicimle = TamSayi & "." & ondalik
    End If
End Function
Function SifirEkle(Sayi, OndalikHane)
    Sayi = CStr(Sayi)
    If OndalikHane - Len(Sayi) > 0 Then
        SifirEkle = Sayi & String(OndalikHane - Len(Sayi), "0")
    Else
        SifirEkle = Sayi
    End If
End Function
Function SifirSil(Sayi)
    Sayi = Trim(CStr(Sayi))
    While Right(Sayi, 1) = "0"
        Sayi = Left(Sayi, Len(Sayi) - 1)
    Wend
    SifirSil = Sayi
End Function
Function VirgulEkle(Sayi)
    If Sayi = 0 Then Exit Function
    Sayi = CStr(Sayi)
    k = Len(Sayi)
    For i = 0 To k - 1
        If i > 0 And i Mod 3 = 0 Then
            Sagdan = "," & Sagdan
        End If
        Sagdan = Mid(Sayi, k - i, 1) & Sagdan
    Next
    VirgulEkle = Sagdan
End Function
Function DecimalSayisi(Sayi)
	sayi = Cstr(Sayi)
    Nokta = InStr(1, Sayi, ".")
    If Nokta = 0 Then
		DecimalSayisi = 0
    Else
		ondalik = Right(CStr(Sayi), Len(CStr(Sayi)) - Nokta)
		DecimalSayisi = len(SifirSil(Ondalik))
    End If
End Function
Function DecimalHesapla(Table,Degisken)
k = 0
DecCount = 0
	For i=1 To Table.GetRecCount()
		DecCount = DecimalSayisi(Table.GetFieldValue(Degisken,i))
		If DecCount >= k Then
			k = DecCount
		End If
	Next
DecimalHesapla = k
End Function
Function NumerikMi(Table,DegiskenAdi)
	For i = 1 To Table.GetRecCount()
		k = Table.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,i)
		If Not(Isnumeric(k)) Then
			NumerikMi = 0
			Exit For
		End If
	NumerikMi=1
	Next
End Function

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public xSutun1, xSutun2, xSutun3, xSutun4, xSutun5, xSutun6, xSutun7, xSutun8, xSutun9, xSutun10
Public wSutun1, wSutun2, wSutun3, wSutun4, wSutun5, wSutun6, wSutun7, wSutun8, wSutun9, wSutun10

Sub PrintForm()
     Dim tableE, tableT
	ReDim AlanSayisi(100)		 
	ReDim xCizgi(100)
	ReDim Secilenler(100,3)
'	Set xCizgi = Nothing
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0

      sayfaTuru = InputBox("Sayfa Türünü Giriniz" & Chr(13) & "A - A4, L - Letter (Sürekli Form)", "Dinamo", "A")

      Select Case UCase(sayfaTuru)
              Case "L"
                      sayfaBoyu = 279.4
                      sayfaEni = 215.9
              Case "A"
                      sayfaBoyu = 297
                      sayfaEni = 210
              Case Else
                      K = MsgBox("A veya L giriniz", 48, "Uyarý")
      End Select					
        Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

        	Set tableE = doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
        	Set tableT = doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
		Call Alanlar(TableE,TableT)
		If ToplamEn > 1.1 * SayfaEni Then
			k = SayfaBoyu
			l = SayfaEni
			SayfaEni = k
			SayfaBoyu = l
			Tip = "LANDSCAPE"
			res = pe1.Open(0, "Rulo Kesim Fiþi", SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu, Tip)
		Else
			Tip = ""
			res = pe1.Open(0, "Rulo Kesim Fiþi", SayfaBoyu,SayfaEni , Tip)
		End If		

		BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
		satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 20

 		satirBaslangic = 70

		If doc.ErrorNumber Then
          	MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
			Exit Sub
        	End If
	Dim pe1
        siraNo = 0        
        Dim satirSayisi_tableT
        satirSayisi_tableT = tableT.GetRecCount()
        Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, tableE)
        For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
                Call Print_form_satir(pe1, tableT, I, tableE)
        Next
        
        Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1, tableE)

        res = pe1.Show()
        res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

        res = pe1.BarCode(kBasla + 4, SayfaEni - 62, kAra, 50, "CODE39", "KF 10-02", "")

        res = pe1.nopen()
		If Tip = "LANDSCAPE" Then
	    	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
     		res = pe1.setbold(1)
	       	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
     	   	res = pe1.write(15, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 75, "Rulo Kesim Fiþi")
       	   	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	       	res = pe1.Picture(12, 10, 15, 55, "\d24\images\muhtese.jpg", 18)
		Else
	       	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
     	   	res = pe1.setbold(1)

        res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "12")

		MamulAmbarKodu = evrak.GetFieldValue("AMBCODE",1)
		MamulAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",MamulAmbarKodu,"")  
		ImAmbarKodu = evrak.GetFieldValue("IMALATAMBCODE",1)
		ImAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",ImAmbarKodu,"")
